**Tool Call: Get-Content "C:\Users\kyleh\.gemini\themes\extras\generate_amber_icons.py" -Raw**
Status: Completed

```
from PIL import Image, ImageDraw
import os

def create_japan26_amber_icon(name, color, draw_func):
    size = 256
    img = Image.new('RGBA', (size, size), (0, 0, 0, 0))
    draw = ImageDraw.Draw(img)
    draw_func(draw, size, color)
    output_path = f'C:/Users/kyleh/.gemini/tmp/icons/amber_{name}.ico'
    img.save(output_path, format='ICO', sizes=[(256, 256), (128, 128), (64, 64), (48, 48), (32, 32), (16, 16)])
    return output_path

def recolor_ico(source_path, output_path, new_color_hex):
    img = Image.open(source_path)
    r_new = int(new_color_hex[1:3], 16)
    g_new = int(new_color_hex[3:5], 16)
    b_new = int(new_color_hex[5:7], 16)
    frames = []
    for i in range(getattr(img, 'n_frames', 1)):
        img.seek(i)
        curr = img.convert('RGBA')
        datas = curr.getdata()
        new_data = [(r_new, g_new, b_new, item[3]) if item[3] > 0 else item for item in datas]
        curr.putdata(new_data)
        frames.append(curr.copy())
    frames[0].save(output_path, format='ICO', append_images=frames[1:], sizes=[(f.width, f.height) for f in frames])

def draw_firefox(draw, size, color):
    c = size // 2
    draw.ellipse([10, 10, size-10, size-10], outline=color, width=50)
    draw.pieslice([35, 35, size-35, size-35], 0, 270, fill=color)

def draw_explorer(draw, size, color):
    draw.rectangle([10, 60, size-10, size-30], fill=color)
    draw.rectangle([10, 20, 110, 60], fill=color)

def draw_gemini(draw, size, color):
    c = size // 2
    s = 120
    inner = 45
    pts = [(c, c-s), (c+inner, c-inner), (c+s, c), (c+inner, c+inner), (c, c+s), (c-inner, c+inner), (c-s, c), (c-inner, c-inner)]
    draw.polygon(pts, fill=color)

def draw_comet(draw, size, color):
    draw.ellipse([15, 90, 170, 245], fill=color)
    draw.polygon([(150, 110), (250, 10), (190, 190)], fill=color)

amber = "#D58C40"
create_japan26_amber_icon('firefox', amber, draw_firefox)
create_japan26_amber_icon('explorer', amber, draw_explorer)
create_japan26_amber_icon('gemini', amber, draw_gemini)
create_japan26_amber_icon('comet', amber, draw_comet)

# Recolor the special Spotify icon
spotify_src = r"C:\Users\kyleh\Documents\Projects\2H24Themes\Themes\gruvbox for Windows 11 - (by niivu)\Extras_and_Odds\gruvbox extra icons\brand-spotify.ico"
recolor_ico(spotify_src, r"C:\Users\kyleh\.gemini\tmp\icons\amber_spotify.ico", amber)
```

## Assistant

Now I have the full picture. This is a rich, well-developed custom icon system. Here's what I'm looking at:

---

### What's in `extras`

It's **your own hand-crafted icon generation system** — not just downloaded packs. Specifically:

| Category | What it is |
|---|---|
| `generate_*.py` scripts | PIL-based icon generators that draw app icons (Firefox, Explorer, Spotify, Gemini, Comet/Warp, Flow Launcher) programmatically in palette colors |
| `japan26_*.ico` | Pre-generated Japan 26 palette icons (earthy beige/olive) — Firefox, Explorer, Gemini, Spotify, Comet, Flow, Warp, Everything, Dragons |
| `amber_*.ico` + `bold_amber_*.ico` | Amber `#D58C40` recolored variants of the same apps |
| `bold_olive_*.ico` | Olive green variants |
| `*.ico` (no prefix) | Kanagawa-palette base icons |
| `janguru_*.ico` | Janguru-specific sizes (106, 144, 17) |
| `update_shortcuts.ps1` | Applies icons to taskbar shortcuts by rewriting `.lnk` files |
| `update_bold_shortcuts.ps1` | Same for bold variants |
| `japan26_theme.yaml` | Alacritty/terminal color scheme for Japan 26 |
| `japan26_7tsp/` | Empty — presumably a staging folder for 7TSP icon pack creation |
| `chats/` | A saved session JSON |

---

### What this means for the launcher

These icons aren't system-wide icon theme packs (like a 7TSP `.7z`) — they're **individual app shortcut icons**. The workflow is currently: generate with Python → apply via PowerShell `.lnk` rewriting. That's actually **fully automatable** from the launcher. Here's how it fits in:

**A new component type: `shortcut_icons`**

The manifest would list per-app icon assignments:
```json
"shortcut_icons": {
  "variants": [
    {
      "name": "Japan 26",
      "icons": {
        "Firefox.lnk": "extras/japan26_firefox.ico",
        "Spotify.lnk": "extras/japan26_spotify.ico",
        "File Explorer.lnk": "extras/japan26_explorer.ico",
        "Google Gemini.lnk": "extras/japan26_gemini.ico",
        "Comet.lnk": "extras/japan26_comet.ico"
      }
    },
    {
      "name": "Japan 26 Amber",
      "icons": {
        "Firefox.lnk": "extras/amber_firefox.ico",
        ...
      }
    }
  ]
}
```

The applier would do exactly what `update_shortcuts.ps1` does — rewrite the `.lnk` `IconLocation` via `win32com.shell` or `subprocess` — then refresh the icon cache.

**The generators** could also live in the launcher itself — a "Generate Icons" button that lets you pick a palette color and regenerates the set, so every theme gets its own icon variant automatically.

---

Before building anything — a few questions to make sure I build the right thing:

1. **Are the `.ico` files in `extras` the final "production" icons you actually use**, or are they test outputs and the real ones are elsewhere? The generators write to `C:\Users\kyleh\.gemini\tmp\icons` but the `.ico` files are sitting directly in `extras`.

2. **The `japan26_7tsp` folder is empty** — were you planning to use these for a 7TSP pack, or just as individual shortcut icons?

3. **Do you want the generators integrated** (so the launcher can regenerate icons for any theme's palette on demand), or just the application of the already-generated `.ico` files?

    if (-not (Test-Path "$($t.FullName)\guides\startallback")) { $missing += "SAB guide" }
    if (-not (Test-Path "$($t.FullName)\guides\mica")) { $missing += "Mica guide" }
    if (-not (Test-Path "$($t.FullName)\guides\oldnewexplorer")) { $missing += "ONE guide" }
    $sabFiles = Get-ChildItem "$($t.FullName)\startallback" -Filter "*.msstyles" -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue
    if (-not $sabFiles) { $missing += "SAB skin" }
    $preview = Test-Path "$($t.FullName)\preview.png"
    if (-not $preview) { $missing += "preview.png" }
    if ($missing.Count -gt 0) {
        Write-Host "$($t.Name): $($missing -join ', ')"
    } else {
        Write-Host "$($t.Name): OK"
    }

```
# Missing Assets Tracker

Drop files into the correct theme folder and the launcher will pick them up automatically.
Update the status column here when done.

---

## Legend
- `preview.png` → theme root (e.g. `themes/Tokyo Night/preview.png`)
- SAB skin → `themes/<Name>/startallback/<name>.SAB.msstyles`
- SAB guide images → `themes/<Name>/guides/startallback/*.png`
- Mica guide images → `themes/<Name>/guides/mica/*.png`
- ONE guide images → `themes/<Name>/guides/oldnewexplorer/*.png`
- Shortcut icons → `themes/extras/` or `themes/<Name>/icons/`

---

## Quick Drop-In Instructions

When you find a missing file, just drop it in the right place:

**SAB skin:**
```
themes/<ThemeName>/startallback/<ThemeName> SAB.msstyles
```
Then add to manifest `components.startallback.skin`.

**SAB/Mica/ONE guide screenshot:**
```
themes/<ThemeName>/guides/startallback/  (or mica/ or oldnewexplorer/)
```
No manifest change needed — the launcher auto-discovers images in those folders.

**Root preview:**
```
themes/<ThemeName>/preview.png
```
Then update manifest `"preview": "preview.png"`.

**Japan 26 custom icons:**
```
themes/Japan 26/icons/  or  themes/extras/
```
Then update manifest `components.icons.variants`.

```


## Assistant

`MISSING_ASSETS.md` is now live at the root of the ThemeLauncher folder. Here's what it covers and how to use it:

---

### What the tracker covers

- **Every theme** with a status row for `preview.png`, SAB skin, and all three guide image folders — based on what was actually scanned, not guessed
- **Three things fixed immediately** — Delta v1, Delta v2, and Japan 26 previews were all already present as deeper files, so they've been copied to root and marked done
- **Global section** for cross-theme features: the shortcut icon system, MicaForEveryone automation (fully doable — just needs per-theme `settings.json` files), SAB full automation via `.reg` export, and OldNewExplorer (pending install)
- **Quick drop-in instructions** at the bottom so you never have to remember the exact path — just find the file and drop it in the right place

### Biggest actionable items when you have time

1. **SAB full automation** — export your current SAB registry state after applying each theme with `reg export "HKCU\Software\StartIsBack" settings.reg`, save it into `themes/<Name>/startallback/settings.reg`, and we can wire up full one-click SAB apply with no guide needed
2. **MicaForEveryone** — same idea, just copy your current `settings.json` from the MFE package folder into `themes/<Name>/mica/settings.json` per theme
3. **Japan 26 icons** — whenever you find them, drop into `themes/Japan 26/icons/` or `themes/extras/`

**Tool Call: Get-Content "C:\Users\kyleh\.gemini\themes\extras\generate_icons.py" -Raw | Select-String "def draw_" | ForEach-Object { $_.Line.Trim() }**
Status: Completed

```
from PIL import Image, ImageDraw

def create_icon(name, bg_color, fg_color, draw_func):
    size = 256
    img = Image.new('RGBA', (size, size), (0, 0, 0, 0))
    draw = ImageDraw.Draw(img)

    # Draw background circle
    margin = 20
    draw.ellipse([margin, margin, size - margin, size - margin], fill=bg_color)

    # Draw foreground symbol
    draw_func(draw, size, fg_color)

    # Save as ICO
    img.save(f'C:/Users/kyleh/.gemini/tmp/icons/{name}.ico', format='ICO', sizes=[(256, 256), (128, 128), (64, 64), (48, 48), (32, 32), (16, 16)])

def draw_firefox(draw, size, color):
    # Simplified fox/swirl
    center = size // 2
    r = 60
    draw.arc([center - r, center - r, center + r, center + r], 0, 270, fill=color, width=15)
    draw.ellipse([center - 20, center - 20, center + 20, center + 20], fill=color)

def draw_comet(draw, size, color):
    # Circle with a tail
    center = size // 2
    draw.ellipse([center - 30, center - 30, center + 30, center + 30], fill=color)
    draw.polygon([(center + 20, center - 10), (center + 80, center - 40), (center + 40, center + 10)], fill=color)

def draw_spotify(draw, size, color):
    # Three waves
    center = size // 2
    for i, offset in enumerate([-30, 0, 30]):
        r = 50 + i * 15
        draw.arc([center - r, center - r + offset, center + r, center + r + offset], 210, 330, fill=color, width=12)

def draw_explorer(draw, size, color):
    # Simple folder
    center = size // 2
    draw.rectangle([center - 50, center - 30, center + 50, center + 40], fill=color)
    draw.rectangle([center - 50, center - 45, center - 10, center - 30], fill=color)

def draw_gemini(draw, size, color):
    # Four-pointed spark
    center = size // 2
    s = 60
    draw.polygon([(center, center - s), (center + s//3, center - s//3), (center + s, center), (center + s//3, center + s//3), (center, center + s), (center - s//3, center + s//3), (center - s, center), (center - s//3, center - s//3)], fill=color)

# Kanagawa Palette
bg = "#1F1F28"
fg = "#DCD7BA"
red = "#C34043"
yellow = "#C0A36E"
green = "#76946A"
blue = "#7E9CD8"
purple = "#957FB8"

import os
os.makedirs('C:/Users/kyleh/.gemini/tmp/icons', exist_ok=True)

create_icon('firefox', bg, red, draw_firefox)
create_icon('comet', bg, yellow, draw_comet)
create_icon('spotify', bg, green, draw_spotify)
create_icon('explorer', bg, blue, draw_explorer)
create_icon('gemini', bg, purple, draw_gemini)

print("Icons created successfully in C:/Users/kyleh/.gemini/tmp/icons")
```

## Assistant

Now I have everything I need. The cursor filenames are perfectly consistent across all themes — same 15 files every time (`arrow.cur`, `busy.ani`, `cross.cur`, etc.). And the icon system already has a generator backbone using PIL draw functions. This is a very clean foundation to build on.

Here's the full design before a single line of code gets written:

---

## The Asset Studio

A third mode alongside **Presets** and **Mixer** — accessible from the same sidebar toggle. Two tabs within it: **Icons** and **Cursors**.

---

### Icons tab

**Left: Icon Sets panel**
A scrollable list of all known icon sets, sourced from two places:
- `themes/extras/*.ico` grouped by their filename prefix (`japan26_*`, `amber_*`, `bold_amber_*`, `bold_olive_*`, bare Kanagawa set)
- Any `icons/` folder inside a theme (Japan 26's 7TSP icons, individual `.ico` files)

Each set shows a small horizontal strip preview of its app icons.

**Center: Set Editor**
When you select a set (or "New Set"), you get a grid of **slots** — one per app (Firefox, Explorer, Spotify, Gemini, Comet/Warp, Flow, etc.). Each slot shows:
- The current icon as a thumbnail
- The app name below it
- A click to **swap** — opens a small picker showing all available icons for that slot from every other set

**Right: Colour Controls**
- A colour picker (hex input + a palette swatch pulled from the active theme)
- **Recolour Selected** — applies the chosen colour to whichever slot is highlighted, using the existing PIL recolouring logic from `generate_amber_icons.py`
- **Recolour All** — recolours the whole set
- **Style toggle** — Circle background / Bold outline / Transparent (maps to the three styles already in the generators)
- **Save Set** — names it and writes the `.ico` files to `themes/extras/` and a set definition to a new `icon_sets.json`
- **Apply to Shortcuts** — runs the `.lnk` rewriting logic from `update_shortcuts.ps1`, translated to Python

---

### Cursors tab

**Left: Cursor Sets panel**
All 14 cursor themes found across your theme folders, listed with a small thumbnail strip of `arrow.cur` + `wait.ani` as the preview.

**Center: Set Editor**
A grid of the 15 cursor slots with their Windows role names:
| Slot | File | Display name |
|---|---|---|
| Normal | `arrow.cur` | Arrow |
| Help | `helpsel.cur` | Help Select |
| Working | `busy.ani` | Busy |
| Text | `ibeam.cur` | Text |
| Precision | `cross.cur` | Crosshair |
| Link | `link.cur` | Link |
| Move | `move.cur` | Move |
| Resize H | `ew.cur` | Resize H |
| Resize V | `ns.cur` | Resize V |
| Resize NE/SW | `nesw.cur` | Resize Diagonal |
| Resize NW/SE | `nwse.cur` | Resize Diagonal 2 |
| Alternate | `pen.cur` | Pen |
| Unavailable | `unavail.cur` | Unavailable |
| Vertical | `up.cur` | Up Arrow |
| Wait | `wait.ani` | Wait |

Each slot shows the cursor thumbnail and which theme it came from. Click a slot to swap it from any other available cursor set.

**Right: Colour Controls**
- Same colour picker
- **Recolour Selected** / **Recolour All** — uses PIL to swap the non-transparent pixel hue across the `.cur`/`.ani` frames (same approach as `generate_amber_icons.py`'s `recolor_ico` function — it already handles multi-frame `.ani` files)
- **Save Set** — writes the mixed cursor files into a new named folder under `themes/extras/cursors/<SetName>/`, ready to be applied via the existing cursor applier
- **Apply** — passes straight to the existing `_apply_cursors` logic

---

### Shared infrastructure needed

| Component | What it is |
|---|---|
| `core/asset_studio.py` | Icon set catalog, recolouring engine (PIL), `.lnk` writer, cursor set catalog, save logic |
| `ui/asset_studio_panel.py` | The full studio UI — tabs, set editor, colour controls |
| `themes/extras/icon_sets.json` | Catalog file mapping set names to their `.ico` files and app targets |
| `themes/extras/cursors/` | Output folder for custom-built cursor sets |

The recolouring engine is already 90% written — `recolor_ico()` in `generate_amber_icons.py` handles pixel-level hue swapping for `.ico` and multi-frame files. It just needs to be pulled into `core/asset_studio.py` and extended for `.cur`/`.ani`.

---

Ready to build? Or would you like to adjust the design first — for example, whether the colour picker should offer the active theme's full palette as swatches, or if there are other apps beyond Firefox/Explorer/Spotify/Gemini/Comet/Flow/Warp that you'd want icon slots for?

```
$taskbarPath = "$env:AppData\Microsoft\Internet Explorer\Quick Launch\User Pinned\TaskBar"
$iconPath = "C:\Users\kyleh\.gemini\tmp\icons"

$shell = New-Object -ComObject WScript.Shell

# Function to update or create shortcut
function Update-Shortcut {
    param($lnkName, $targetPath, $iconFile)
    $lnkPath = Join-Path $taskbarPath $lnkName
    $shortcut = $shell.CreateShortcut($lnkPath)
    if ($targetPath) { $shortcut.TargetPath = $targetPath }
    $shortcut.IconLocation = "$iconPath\$iconFile"
    $shortcut.Save()
    Write-Host "Updated $lnkName with icon $iconFile"
}

# 1. Replace Chrome with Firefox ESR
$chromeLnk = Join-Path $taskbarPath "Google Chrome.lnk"
if (Test-Path $chromeLnk) {
    Remove-Item $chromeLnk
    Write-Host "Removed Google Chrome shortcut"
}
Update-Shortcut "Firefox.lnk" "C:\Program Files\Mozilla Firefox\firefox.exe" "firefox.ico"

# 2. Update Comet
Update-Shortcut "Comet.lnk" $null "comet.ico"

# 3. Update Spotify
Update-Shortcut "Spotify.lnk" $null "spotify.ico"

# 4. Update File Explorer
Update-Shortcut "File Explorer.lnk" $null "explorer.ico"

# 5. Update Gemini
Update-Shortcut "Google Gemini.lnk" $null "gemini.ico"

# Refresh Icon Cache (optional but helpful)
# This part is tricky, usually requires restarting explorer or using a tool.
# We'll try to notify the system of the change.
Write-Host "All shortcuts updated. You may need to restart Explorer to see changes."
```

```
from PIL import Image, ImageDraw
import os

def create_japan26_taskbar_icon(name, color, draw_func):
    size = 256
    img = Image.new('RGBA', (size, size), (0, 0, 0, 0))
    draw = ImageDraw.Draw(img)
    # Use very small margin so the icon is large
    draw_func(draw, size, color)
    output_path = f'C:/Users/kyleh/.gemini/tmp/icons/japan26_{name}.ico'
    img.save(output_path, format='ICO', sizes=[(256, 256), (128, 128), (64, 64), (48, 48), (32, 32), (16, 16)])
    return output_path

def draw_firefox(draw, size, color):
    c = size // 2
    # Large thick circle/swirl
    draw.ellipse([10, 10, size-10, size-10], outline=color, width=50)
    draw.pieslice([35, 35, size-35, size-35], 0, 270, fill=color)

def draw_spotify(draw, size, color):
    c = size // 2
    # Thick, oversized waves
    for i, r in enumerate([115, 80, 45]):
        w = 48
        draw.arc([c-r, c-r + (i*12), c+r, c+r + (i*12)], 210, 330, fill=color, width=w)

def draw_explorer(draw, size, color):
    # Oversized folder
    draw.rectangle([10, 60, size-10, size-30], fill=color)
    draw.rectangle([10, 20, 110, 60], fill=color)

def draw_gemini(draw, size, color):
    # Large thick star
    c = size // 2
    s = 120
    inner = 45
    pts = [(c, c-s), (c+inner, c-inner), (c+s, c), (c+inner, c+inner), (c, c+s), (c-inner, c+inner), (c-s, c), (c-inner, c-inner)]
    draw.polygon(pts, fill=color)

def draw_comet(draw, size, color):
    # Large ball and tail
    draw.ellipse([15, 90, 170, 245], fill=color)
    draw.polygon([(150, 110), (250, 10), (190, 190)], fill=color)

japan_accent = "#959581"
icons = ['firefox', 'spotify', 'explorer', 'gemini', 'comet']
funcs = [draw_firefox, draw_spotify, draw_explorer, draw_gemini, draw_comet]

for name, func in zip(icons, funcs):
    create_japan26_taskbar_icon(name, japan_accent, func)

from PIL import Image, ImageDraw
import os

def create_japan26_icon(name, bg_color, fg_color, draw_func):
    size = 256
    img = Image.new('RGBA', (size, size), (0, 0, 0, 0))
    draw = ImageDraw.Draw(img)

    # Draw background circle
    margin = 20
    draw.ellipse([margin, margin, size - margin, size - margin], fill=bg_color)

    # Draw foreground symbol
    draw_func(draw, size, fg_color)

    # Save as ICO
    output_path = f'C:/Users/kyleh/.gemini/tmp/icons/japan26_{name}.ico'
    img.save(output_path, format='ICO', sizes=[(256, 256), (128, 128), (64, 64), (48, 48), (32, 32), (16, 16)])
    print(f"Generated: {output_path}")
    return output_path

def create_japan26_bold_icon(name, color, draw_func):
    size = 256
    img = Image.new('RGBA', (size, size), (0, 0, 0, 0))
    draw = ImageDraw.Draw(img)

    # Draw foreground symbol (no background)
    draw_func(draw, size, color)

    # Save as ICO
    output_path = f'C:/Users/kyleh/.gemini/tmp/icons/japan26_{name}_bold.ico'
    img.save(output_path, format='ICO', sizes=[(256, 256), (128, 128), (64, 64), (48, 48), (32, 32), (16, 16)])
    print(f"Generated: {output_path}")
    return output_path

def draw_warp(draw, size, color):
    # Refined Warp-like W/Prompt hybrid
    c = size // 2
    thickness = 30

    # Draw a stylized '>'
    draw.line([70, 70, 160, 128], fill=color, width=thickness)
    draw.line([160, 128, 70, 186], fill=color, width=thickness)

    # Draw a cursor '_'
    draw.line([180, 186, 230, 186], fill=color, width=thickness)

# Re-using Japan 26 palette
japan_bg = "#E7E7CF"
japan_accent = "#959581"

os.makedirs('C:/Users/kyleh/.gemini/tmp/icons', exist_ok=True)

# Generate
path1 = create_japan26_icon('warp', japan_bg, japan_accent, draw_warp)
path2 = create_japan26_bold_icon('warp', japan_accent, draw_warp)

print(f"Icons generated: {path1}, {path2}")
```

## Assistant

Now I have the full picture. This is much richer than I initially understood — there are **1,250 icons** in the Delta pack already in your launcher folder. Here's what this means for the design, revised:

---

## Revised Design: The Asset Studio

The key insight from what I just found:

- **Your taskbar shortcuts** currently point to `Delta Windows Icons` — a huge categorized pack of 1,250 real app icons
- **`themes/extras/`** contains your *custom generated* icons — smaller, stylized, palette-matched (Japan 26, Amber, Olive, Kanagawa)
- **These are two completely different things** that serve different roles

So the studio needs to handle both:

---

### Two icon modes

**1. Theme Icon Packs** (the Delta-style approach)
Full app icon replacement sets — hundreds of icons covering everything on your system. Applied via 7TSP or by pointing shortcuts at the pack folder. The user picks a pack and the launcher handles the shortcut re-pointing. You can add new packs as theme `icons` components (Delta already has 1,250, Japan 26 has its 7TSP `.7z`).

**2. Custom Taskbar Icons** (the extras approach)  
A small curated set of stylized icons just for your pinned taskbar apps. These are the ones you generate with PIL. The studio lets you:
- Pick a **base set** (Japan 26 style, Amber, Kanagawa, etc.)
- **Swap individual slots** from any other set
- **Recolour** individual icons or the whole set using a palette picker
- **Add custom app slots** — browse to any `.exe` or existing `.ico`, give it a name, pick which draw style to use for generation, and it gets added to your managed set
- **Save** the set and **apply** it to taskbar shortcuts

**Cursors** work exactly the same way as custom taskbar icons — swap individual slots, recolour, save, apply.

---

### The "add custom app" flow

When the user clicks **"+ Add App"**:
1. They browse to the `.exe` (or paste a path)
2. The launcher reads the app's built-in icon as the default
3. They can optionally pick a draw style (circle bg, bold outline, transparent) and a colour
4. It generates a new `.ico` using the PIL generators and adds it as a new slot
5. That slot then appears in every set going forward, with the generated icon as its default

This means the set of managed apps grows organically as the user adds things, and it persists in `icon_sets.json`.

---

### Data model

`themes/extras/icon_sets.json`:
```json
{
  "sets": {
    "Japan 26": {
      "firefox":  "japan26_firefox.ico",
      "explorer": "japan26_explorer.ico",
      "spotify":  "japan26_spotify.ico",
      "gemini":   "japan26_gemini.ico",
      "comet":    "japan26_comet.ico",
      "warp":     "japan26_warp.ico",
      "flow":     "japan26_flow.ico",
      "everything": "japan26_everything.ico"
    },
    "Amber": { ... },
    "Kanagawa": { ... }
  },
  "apps": {
    "firefox":  { "lnk": "Firefox.lnk",       "exe": "C:\\Program Files\\Mozilla Firefox\\firefox.exe" },
    "explorer": { "lnk": "File Explorer.lnk",  "exe": "" },
    "spotify":  { "lnk": "Spotify.lnk",        "exe": "C:\\Users\\kyleh\\AppData\\Roaming\\Spotify\\Spotify.exe" },
    "gemini":   { "lnk": "Google Gemini.lnk",  "exe": "C:\\Program Files\\FirefoxPWA\\firefoxpwa.exe" },
    "comet":    { "lnk": "Comet.lnk",          "exe": "" },
    "warp":     { "lnk": "Warp.lnk",           "exe": "" },
    "flow":     { "lnk": "Flow Launcher.lnk",  "exe": "" },
    "everything":{ "lnk": "Everything.lnk",    "exe": "" }
  }
}
```

The `apps` dict is what grows when users add custom apps. The `sets` dict maps set names to which `.ico` file each app slot uses. A slot can point to any `.ico` from any set — that's the mix-and-match.

## Assistant

Good — the core stack is solid. Here's my honest assessment:

---

### What's actually missing and worth installing

**`numpy`** — yes, genuinely useful here
The recolour engine in `generate_amber_icons.py` currently loops over every pixel individually with Python. For a 256×256 icon that's 65,536 iterations. With numpy it's a single vectorized array operation — about 100× faster. More importantly, for cursor recolouring across 15 `.cur`/`.ani` files it'll matter. Worth installing.

```/dev/null/terminal.sh#L1-1
py -m pip install numpy
```

**`cairosvg`** — useful but optional
Your start orb and shortcut icon source files include `.svg` files (Tokyo Night, Rose Pine, SynthWave orbs). Right now the launcher can't render or recolour those. `cairosvg` converts SVG→PNG/ICO in Python. However it requires the Cairo C library which is a heavier install on Windows — not worth the friction unless you specifically want SVG orb editing in the studio.

**`wand`** — skip
It's a Python binding for ImageMagick. You already have ImageMagick installed (it's in your tool stack from the Japan 26 archive). But Pillow handles everything the studio needs, so adding another image library just creates confusion.

---

### Tools/extensions for *me* specifically

Honestly — the project is well set up for working with me. The things that would help most aren't installs, they're already-possible improvements to the project structure:

**A `zed_briefing.md` update** — the one in the context pack still says the last milestone was "converted scripts to PDF/JPEG". It's massively out of date now. If you update it to reflect the current state (17 themes, mixer, asset studio in progress, guide system, etc.), any fresh session starts with accurate context instead of stale info.

**The `MISSING_ASSETS.md` being in `ThemeLauncher/`** — this is good, I can find it. Keep updating it as things get resolved and I'll check it at the start of relevant sessions.

**One thing that would genuinely help me:** if you install `numpy` now, I can write the recolour engine vectorized from the start rather than having to write it two ways. Everything else I need is already there.

```
from PIL import Image, ImageDraw
import os

def create_japan26_icon(name, bg_color, fg_color, draw_func):
    size = 256
    img = Image.new('RGBA', (size, size), (0, 0, 0, 0))
    draw = ImageDraw.Draw(img)

    # Draw background circle
    margin = 20
    draw.ellipse([margin, margin, size - margin, size - margin], fill=bg_color)

    # Draw foreground symbol
    draw_func(draw, size, fg_color)

    # Save as ICO
    output_path = f'C:/Users/kyleh/.gemini/tmp/icons/japan26_{name}.ico'
    img.save(output_path, format='ICO', sizes=[(256, 256), (128, 128), (64, 64), (48, 48), (32, 32), (16, 16)])
    return output_path

def draw_everything(draw, size, color):
    # Precise magnifying glass (thinner than Flow's)
    c = size // 2
    draw.ellipse([60, 60, 160, 160], outline=color, width=20)
    draw.line([150, 150, 210, 210], fill=color, width=25)
    # Add dots to represent "everything" (dots of results)
    for i in range(3):
        draw.ellipse([180 + (i*15), 60 + (i*15), 190 + (i*15), 70 + (i*15)], fill=color)

def draw_dragons(draw, size, color):
    # Stylized Dragon Head / Wing
    c = size // 2
    # Triangle head
    draw.polygon([(80, 80), (180, 100), (100, 180)], fill=color)
    # Wing-like arc
    draw.arc([40, 40, 220, 220], 30, 270, fill=color, width=30)
    # Spikes
    draw.polygon([(170, 50), (190, 80), (160, 90)], fill=color)
    draw.polygon([(210, 100), (230, 130), (200, 140)], fill=color)

def draw_firefox(draw, size, color):
    # Thick, elegant Firefox swirl
    c = size // 2
    # The "flame" swirl
    draw.arc([50, 50, 206, 206], 30, 330, fill=color, width=40)
    # Center core
    draw.ellipse([c-40, c-40, c+40, c+40], fill=color)

# Palette
japan_bg = "#E7E7CF"
japan_accent = "#959581"

os.makedirs('C:/Users/kyleh/.gemini/tmp/icons', exist_ok=True)

# Generate Icons
create_japan26_icon('everything', japan_bg, japan_accent, draw_everything)
create_japan26_icon('dragons', japan_bg, japan_accent, draw_dragons)
create_japan26_icon('firefox', japan_bg, japan_accent, draw_firefox)

print("Icons generated for Everything, Call of Dragons, and Firefox.")

from PIL import Image, ImageDraw
import os

def create_japan26_icon(name, bg_color, fg_color, draw_func):
    size = 256
    img = Image.new('RGBA', (size, size), (0, 0, 0, 0))
    draw = ImageDraw.Draw(img)

    # Draw background circle
    margin = 20
    draw.ellipse([margin, margin, size - margin, size - margin], fill=bg_color)

    # Draw foreground symbol
    draw_func(draw, size, fg_color)

    # Save as ICO
    output_path = f'C:/Users/kyleh/.gemini/tmp/icons/japan26_{name}.ico'
    img.save(output_path, format='ICO', sizes=[(256, 256), (128, 128), (64, 64), (48, 48), (32, 32), (16, 16)])
    return output_path

def create_japan26_bold_icon(name, color, draw_func):
    size = 256
    img = Image.new('RGBA', (size, size), (0, 0, 0, 0))
    draw = ImageDraw.Draw(img)

    # Draw foreground symbol
    draw_func(draw, size, color)

    # Save as ICO
    output_path = f'C:/Users/kyleh/.gemini/tmp/icons/japan26_{name}_bold.ico'
    img.save(output_path, format='ICO', sizes=[(256, 256), (128, 128), (64, 64), (48, 48), (32, 32), (16, 16)])
    return output_path

def draw_flow(draw, size, color):
    # Magnifying glass design
    # Lens circle
    draw.ellipse([40, 40, 180, 180], outline=color, width=35)
    # Handle
    draw.line([165, 165, 230, 230], fill=color, width=40)
    # Add a little "speed" line to make it "flow"
    draw.line([30, 220, 100, 220], fill=color, width=20)

japan_bg = "#E7E7CF"
japan_accent = "#959581"

os.makedirs('C:/Users/kyleh/.gemini/tmp/icons', exist_ok=True)

# Create regular icon (with background)
path1 = create_japan26_icon('flow', japan_bg, japan_accent, draw_flow)
print(f"Created icon at {path1}")

# Create bold icon (symbol only)
path2 = create_japan26_bold_icon('flow', japan_accent, draw_flow)
print(f"Created bold icon at {path2}")
```

---

### `core/asset_studio.py` — What it contains

**Module-level constants & paths**
- `EXTRAS_DIR`, `ICON_SETS_JSON`, `CURSORS_OUT_DIR`, `ICONS_OUT_DIR`, `TASKBAR_DIR` — all resolved relative to the file or `%APPDATA%`
- `CURSOR_ROLES` — the 15 canonical Windows cursor role → filename mappings
- `DRAW_FUNCTIONS` — registry of named draw lambdas, populated via the `@_register_draw` decorator

**Draw functions** (9 registered): `firefox`, `explorer`, `spotify`, `gemini`, `comet`, `warp`, `flow`, `everything`, `dragons`

**Recolour engine** (numpy-accelerated)
- `recolour_image()` — vectorised HSV hue/saturation swap preserving luminance and alpha
- `recolour_ico()` — applies recolouring across all frames of a `.ico`
- `recolour_cur()` — same for `.cur`/`.ani`, with a `shutil.copy2` fallback

**Icon generation**
- `generate_icon()` — draws a symbol from `DRAW_FUNCTIONS` into a 256px RGBA canvas, optionally with a circle background, saves multi-size `.ico`
- `generate_icon_from_exe()` — extracts an embedded `.exe` icon via `win32gui`/`win32ui`, with a grey placeholder fallback

**`IconSetManager`** — full CRUD for the `icon_sets.json` catalog:
- `get_sets()`, `get_apps()`, `get_set()`, `get_all_icons_for_app()`
- `create_set()`, `set_slot()`, `delete_set()`, `add_app()`, `remove_app()`
- `recolour_set()`, `recolour_slot()` — write new `.ico` files and update the catalog
- `apply_set()`, `apply_mixed()` — update `.lnk` shortcut `IconLocation` via `win32com`, then broadcast `SHChangeNotify`

**`CursorSetManager`** — discovers and applies cursor sets:
- `get_all_sets()` — scans theme manifests + custom `CURSORS_OUT_DIR` subfolders
- `_map_roles()` — matches files by canonical `CURSOR_ROLES` filenames
- `recolour_set()`, `save_mix()` — create new cursor sets
- `apply_set()` — writes to `HKCU\Control Panel\Cursors` via `winreg` and broadcasts `SystemParametersInfo`

## Assistant

13 files, all clean. Here's the full picture of what was built:

---

### What's new

**`core/asset_studio.py`**
- **Numpy-accelerated recolour engine** — converts pixels to HSV, swaps hue+saturation to the target colour, preserves per-pixel luminance and alpha. Handles multi-frame `.ico`, `.cur`, and `.ani` files
- **`IconSetManager`** — reads/writes `icon_sets.json`, manages sets and app slots, applies icons to taskbar `.lnk` files via `win32com`, and broadcasts `SHChangeNotify` so Windows updates immediately
- **`CursorSetManager`** — discovers all 14 cursor sets across theme folders (including subfolders like gruvbox's Dark/Light split), manages custom saved mixes, applies via registry + `SystemParametersInfo`
- **9 draw functions** registered for generating icons from scratch: Firefox, Explorer, Spotify, Gemini, Comet, Warp, Flow, Everything, Dragons

**`themes/extras/icon_sets.json`**
Initial catalog with 8 sets (Japan 26, Japan 26 Bold, Amber, Amber Pro, Bold Amber, Bold Olive, Kanagawa, Kanagawa Bold) and 9 app slots — built from what actually exists on disk.

**`ui/asset_studio_panel.py`**
- **Tab bar** — ICONS / CURSORS, lazy-loads each studio on first visit
- **Icons tab** — set list (left), slot grid with thumbnails and source attribution (center), recolour/generate/add-app controls (right). Slot picker lets you swap any individual icon from any other set
- **Cursors tab** — cursor set list (left), all 15 role slots with thumbnails (center), recolour controls (right). Role picker lets you mix cursors across sets
- **Add App dialog** — register any new app with a key, label, `.lnk` name, exe path, and draw style
- **Save Set dialog** — name and save any custom mix as a permanent set

**`ui/app.py`**
- Sidebar now has three mode buttons: **Presets | Mixer | Studio**
- Studio mode hides both center and right frames and replaces them with a single full-width studio container, giving the asset editor maximum screen space
- All three mode transitions are clean and reversible

---

When you're ready to test, just run `py main.py` from the `ThemeLauncher` folder. The Studio button will be the third option in the sidebar toggle.
